| `tensura.skill.thought_communication` | Thought Communication |
| `tensura.skill.thought_communication.description` | Send commands to nearby allies and become able to instruct them to attack players. |
| `tensura.skill.thrower` | Thrower |

| `tensura.skill.unpredictability` | Unpredictability |
| `tensura.skill.unpredictability.description` | In the hands of a True Hero, unpredictability reigns supreme. The Hero sees all, becoming able to ignore dodge and even read the movements of their opponent to disable their critical attacks or their dodge avoidance. |
| `tensura.skill.unyielding` | Unyielding |

| `tensura.skill.usurper` | Usurper |
| `tensura.skill.usurper.description` | Seize your enemies' power or their summons. Steal their abilities and take control of their skills, draining their strength for your own. |
| `tensura.skill.villain` | Villain |
| `tensura.skill.villain.description` | Embody malice and fight on the side of evil. Gain increased power from killing your foes, manipulate your enemies into joining your side and empower your allies. |
| `tensura.skill.violent_break` | Violent Break |
| `tensura.skill.violent_break.description` | Channel your aura, recklessly enhancing your strength and cleansing you of any negative effects. |

| `tensura.skill.warp_portal` | Warp Portal |
| `tensura.skill.warp_portal.description` | Tear space asunder connecting two points in space. |

| `tensura.race.ancient_giant` | Ancient Giant |
| `tensura.race.ancient_giant.description` | Ancient form of Giants with even more incredible physical capabilities and abilities. |
| `tensura.race.arch_daemon` | Arch Daemon |
| `tensura.race.arch_daemon.description` | The peak evolutionary stage that can be reached by daemons in the Daemon Realm. Only the most powerful of greater daemons can ascend to this height. |
| `tensura.race.beast_lord` | Beast Lord |
| `tensura.race.beast_lord.description` | Beastfolk that has "evolved the correct way" and became a Demi-Spiritual Lifeform. |
| `tensura.race.beastfolk` | Beastfolk |
| `tensura.race.beastfolk.description` | A race that can freely change between their true animal form and a more a human form. They possess immense physical prowess and regenerative capabilities that let them fight without rest. |

| `tensura.race.daemon_lord` | Daemon Lord |
| `tensura.race.daemon_lord.description` | One of the most powerful evolutionary stages of daemons, achieved only by the most powerful arch daemons. |
| `tensura.race.death_oni` | Death Oni |
| `tensura.race.death_oni.description` | Wicked Oni that has "evolved the correct way" and became a Spiritual Lifeform. |
| `tensura.race.demon_slime` | Demon Slime |
| `tensura.race.demon_slime.description` | The ultimate stage of evolution of Slimes. They evolve to this stage by awakening as a True Demon Lord during the Harvest Festival. |
| `tensura.race.devil_lord` | Devil Lord |
| `tensura.race.devil_lord.description` | The final stage of evolution for daemons. One emerges when a daemon meets all three requirements of a name, material body, and divinity. |
| `tensura.race.divine_beast` | Divine Beast |
| `tensura.race.divine_beast.description` | Beastfolk that achieved divinity, possessing an immortal physical body that will never age. |
| `tensura.race.divine_bird` | Divine Bird |
| `tensura.race.divine_bird.description` | Harpy that achieved divinity, possessing an immortal physical body that will never age. |
| `tensura.race.divine_boar` | Divine Boar |
| `tensura.race.divine_boar.description` | Orc that achieved divinity, possessing an immortal physical body that will never age. |
| `tensura.race.divine_dragon` | Divine Dragon |
| `tensura.race.divine_dragon.description` | Dragonewt that achieved divinity, possessing an immortal physical body that will never age. |
| `tensura.race.divine_dwarf` | Divine Dwarf |
| `tensura.race.divine_dwarf.description` | Dwarf that achieved divinity, possessing an immortal physical body that will never age. |
| `tensura.race.divine_elf` | Divine Elf |
| `tensura.race.divine_elf.description` | Elf that achieved divinity, possessing an immortal physical body that will never age. |
| `tensura.race.divine_fighter` | Divine Fighter |
| `tensura.race.divine_fighter.description` | Wicked Oni that achieved divinity, possessing an immortal physical body that will never age. |
| `tensura.race.divine_fish` | Divine Fish |
| `tensura.race.divine_fish.description` | Merfolk that achieved divinity, possessing an immortal physical body that will never age. |
| `tensura.race.divine_giant` | Divine Giant |
| `tensura.race.divine_giant.description` | A godly race born from the earth itself, possessing an immortal physical body that will never age. |
| `tensura.race.divine_human` | Divine Human |
| `tensura.race.divine_human.description` | Human that achieved divinity, possessing an immortal physical body that will never age. |
| `tensura.race.divine_oni` | Divine Oni |
| `tensura.race.divine_oni.description` | Oni that achieved divinity, possessing an immortal physical body that will never age. |
| `tensura.race.divine_skeleton` | Divine Skeleton |
| `tensura.race.divine_skeleton.description` | Wight that achieved divinity, possessing an immortal physical body that will never age. |
| `tensura.race.divine_vampire` | Divine Vampire |
| `tensura.race.divine_vampire.description` | Vampire that achieved divinity, possessing an immortal physical body that will never age. |
| `tensura.race.dragonewt` | Dragonewt |
| `tensura.race.dragonewt.description` |  The evolved form of Lizardmen and descendants of dragons. They have an average lifespan of about two hundred years. |
| `tensura.race.dwarf` | Dwarf |
| `tensura.race.dwarf.description` | A sprite race descended from earth elementals. They possess an immense will and make for fierce albeit rather short soldiers. |
| `tensura.race.dwarf_saint` | Dwarf Saint |
| `tensura.race.dwarf_saint.description` | Dwarf that has "evolved the correct way" and became a Spiritual Lifeform. |
| `tensura.race.elf` | Elf |
| `tensura.race.elf.description` | A sprite race descended from wind elementals. They possess a fierce talent for elemental magic and are more in tune with nature than most. |
| `tensura.race.elf_saint` | Elf Saint |
| `tensura.race.elf_saint.description` | Elf that has "evolved the correct way" and became a Spiritual Lifeform. |
| `tensura.race.enlightened_dwarf` | Enlightened Dwarf |
| `tensura.race.enlightened_dwarf.description` | Dwarf that has "evolved the correct way" and became a Demi-Spiritual Lifeform. |
| `tensura.race.enlightened_elf` | Enlightened Elf |
| `tensura.race.enlightened_elf.description` | Elf that has "evolved the correct way" and became a Demi-Spiritual Lifeform. |
| `tensura.race.enlightened_hobgoblin` | Enlightened Hobgoblin |
| `tensura.race.enlightened_hobgoblin.description` | Hobgoblin that has "evolved the correct way" and became a Demi-Spiritual Lifeform. |
| `tensura.race.enlightened_human` | Enlightened Human |
| `tensura.race.enlightened_human.description` | Human that has "evolved the correct way" and became a Demi-Spiritual Lifeform. |
| `tensura.race.enlightened_merfolk` | Enlightened Merfolk |
| `tensura.race.enlightened_merfolk.description` | Merfolk that has "evolved the correct way" and became a Demi-Spiritual Lifeform. |
| `tensura.race.enlightened_ogre` | Enlightened Ogre |
| `tensura.race.enlightened_ogre.description` | Ogre that has "evolved the correct way" and became a Demi-Spiritual Lifeform. |
| `tensura.race.ghoul` | Ghoul |
| `tensura.race.ghoul.description` | A vampiric thrall brought about by Blood Raise, highly weakened by sunlight. |
| `tensura.race.giant` | Giant |
| `tensura.race.giant.description` | A race of powerful Majin most notable for their incredible physical capabilities and Magic Resistance. |
| `tensura.race.goblin` | Goblin |
| `tensura.race.goblin.description` | A race of Sprite Demi-Humans. They seem to be descended from the offspring of Dwarves and Oni. |
| `tensura.race.god_slime` | God Slime |
| `tensura.race.god_slime.description` | The divine stage of evolution of Slimes. |
| `tensura.race.greater_daemon` | Greater Daemon |
| `tensura.race.greater_daemon.description` | The daemon race that lesser daemons evolve to when they develop their ego and accumulate enough power. |
| `tensura.race.harpy` | Harpy |
| `tensura.race.harpy.description` | A race of winged Majin with capability to cancel out flight spell and are often referred to as the rulers of the skies. |
| `tensura.race.harpy_queen` | Harpy Queen |
| `tensura.race.harpy_queen.description` | A Queen Species of Harpy with the ability to reproduce through Parthenogenesis or Virgin Birth. |
| `tensura.race.high_orc` | High Orc |
| `tensura.race.high_orc.description` | The evolved form of Orcs. They are smarter than Orcs while preserving the Orc race's special characteristics, with appearance virtually identical to regular Orcs. |
| `tensura.race.hobgoblin` | Hobgoblin |
| `tensura.race.hobgoblin.description` | The evolved form of male Goblins that is ranked D on average. |
| `tensura.race.hobgoblin_saint` | Hobgoblin Saint |
| `tensura.race.hobgoblin_saint.description` | Hobgoblin that has "evolved the correct way" and became a Spiritual Lifeform. |
| `tensura.race.human` | Human |
| `tensura.race.human.description` | A weak but populous race that relies more on technology and numbers than brute force. Their low magicule count makes skills and magic users a rarity among them, instead favouring battlewill. |
| `tensura.race.human_saint` | Human Saint |
| `tensura.race.human_saint.description` | Human that has "evolved the correct way" and became a Spiritual Lifeform. |
| `tensura.race.kijin` | Kijin |
| `tensura.race.kijin.description` | A race of Sprite Demi-Humans descended from Fire Elementals. They have an average lifespan of over a thousand years |
| `tensura.race.lesser_daemon` | Lesser Daemon |
| `tensura.race.lesser_daemon.description` | The lowest level of the daemon race. They spontaneously come into existence within the Daemon Realm where they slowly accumulate experience from fighting and being summoned before eventually evolving into greater daemons. |
| `tensura.race.lizardman` | Lizardman |
| `tensura.race.lizardman.description` | A race of scaled people descended from dragons. Their webbed feet give them an advantage in wet terrain. |
| `tensura.race.merfolk` | Merfolk |
| `tensura.race.merfolk.description` | A sprite race descended from water elementals. Their fish-like bodies give them an insurmountable advantage in water. |
| `tensura.race.merfolk_saint` | Merfolk Saint |
| `tensura.race.merfolk_saint.description` | Merfolk that has "evolved the correct way" and became a Spiritual Lifeform. |
| `tensura.race.metal_slime` | Metal Slime |
| `tensura.race.metal_slime.description` | A slime that has taken in large quantities of Magic Ore. The dissolved ore inside of it provides a strong resistance towards most forms of damage. |
| `tensura.race.mystic_oni` | Mystic Oni |
| `tensura.race.mystic_oni.description` | The result of Kijin fully returning to their roots as Elementals, making them Spiritual Lifeforms. |
| `tensura.race.ogre` | Ogre |
| `tensura.race.ogre.description` | A sprite race descended from fire elementals. They possess immense physical capabilities and a strong Japanese lineage. |
| `tensura.race.orc` | Orc |
| `tensura.race.orc.description` | A race of beastfolk who lost the ability to shift between man and beast, resulting in a permanent mix of the two. Their physical strength is greater than average but pails in comparison to their original strength as beastfolk. |
| `tensura.race.orc_disaster` | Orc Disaster |
| `tensura.race.orc_disaster.description` | The evolution of an Orc Lord that is a Demon Lord Seed. |
| `tensura.race.orc_lord` | Orc Lord |
| `tensura.race.orc_lord.description` | A very rare and powerful member of the Orc race with high intelligence who appears roughly every few centuries. |
| `tensura.race.slime` | Slime |
| `tensura.race.slime.description` | A Spectral race of monster that lacks intelligence and ambition. They’re usually passive but are incredibly ruthless once provoked. |
| `tensura.race.spirit_beast` | Spirit Beast |
| `tensura.race.spirit_beast.description` | Beastfolk that has "evolved the correct way" and became a Spiritual Lifeform. |
| `tensura.race.spirit_bird` | Spirit Bird |
| `tensura.race.spirit_bird.description` | Harpy that has "evolved the correct way" and became a Spiritual Lifeform. |
| `tensura.race.spirit_boar` | Spirit Boar |
| `tensura.race.spirit_boar.description` | Orc that has "evolved the correct way" and became a Spiritual Lifeform. |
| `tensura.race.spirit_oni` | Spirit Oni |
| `tensura.race.spirit_oni.description` | Oni that has "evolved the correct way" and became a Spiritual Lifeform. |
| `tensura.race.spirit_skeleton` | Spirit Skeleton |
| `tensura.race.spirit_skeleton.description` | Wight that has "evolved the correct way" and became a Spiritual Lifeform. |
| `tensura.race.true_dragonewt` | True Dragonewt |
| `tensura.race.true_dragonewt.description` | The evolution of Dragonewts and complete Spiritual Lifeforms. They are the second-highest evolutionary stage of Dragonewt known to exist. |
| `tensura.race.vampire` | Vampire |
| `tensura.race.vampire.description` | A race of Majin with nearly eternal life. Although they can reproduce, they normally abstain. Hosting overwhelming magical energy and vitality, their flesh never decays. |
| `tensura.race.vampire_lord` | Vampire Lord |
| `tensura.race.vampire_lord.description` | A vampire among the loftiest levels of power, comparable to a Demon Lord. |
| `tensura.race.vampire_overcomer` | Vampire Overcomer |
| `tensura.race.vampire_overcomer.description` | Vampires that have evolved to overcome their weakness against the sun. |
| `tensura.race.wicked_oni` | Wicked Oni |
| `tensura.race.wicked_oni.description` | A variant of Oni, possessing an essence that leans closer towards that of a Daemon rather than that of a pure Elemental. |
| `tensura.race.wight` | Wight |
| `tensura.race.wight.description` | A Demi-Spiritual skeletal Undead Monster, highly weakened by sunlight. |
| `tensura.race.wight_king` | Wight King |
| `tensura.race.wight_king.description` | A type of Undead Monster and an evolution of Wights. They possess fearsome power comparable to that of Demon Lord Seeds. |
